# TSUZUKU — Listening report

Generated by `scripts/listening-report.ts` from `public/content` (content `c-22578c2f0f`); the pools are regenerated in memory by the same `buildPools` call `content:assign` writes `listening/pool-NN.json` from. Basis: speech estimate = morae ÷ 7 s + 0.5 s per clip (morae = kana characters with small kana folded; a collocation's morae are estimated as kana characters + 2 per kanji); bytes at 3.46 KB/s (Opus 24 kbps). Design 08 §2 quotes §2 and §3 of this file; §12 quotes §3. Rerun with `node scripts/listening-report.ts` (`--json` prints the same data).

## 1. Pools by station

Own = the station's own `pool-NN.json`; i+0 = every ref in the pools ≤ the station (the ceiling of what a learner who knows the whole allowlist can follow); i+1 = every later ref with exactly one id beyond the station. At runtime `selectPool` applies the learner's live known set, so the Station page shows a live count, never these ceilings. Own-station examples = the V46 census: vocab examples all-known at the station the word is introduced / all examples of that station's items, and the items with none.

## 2. Hours (design 08 §2)

Distinct = each text rendered once; rendered adds the natural-slow second take of the Stations 1–17 sentence clips (pieces through Orange).

| Source | What | Count · distinct audio | Tier |
|---|---|---|---|
| Mined sentences | vocab `examples[]` in the pools (8,710 of 12,775; 15.6 h if all were rendered) | 8,710 · 9.8 h | T2 pool |
| | grammar `examples[]` (1,531 in the pools) | 1,892 · 1.9 h | T1 core |
| | story lines of the texts (3,165 in the pools) | 3,268 · 4.0 h | T1 core |
| | michikusa quotes | 178 · 0.1 h | T1 core |
| Audio-first pieces | 100 texts (`audioFirst`) on disk | 2,911 · 3.9 h | T1 core |
| Drill clips | bare words (by item id) | 6,387 · 1.8 h | T1 core |
| | kanji example words not covered by a vocab clip (1,984 reuse one) | 898 · 0.3 h | T1 core |
| | kana + `exampleWord` | 476 · 0.1 h | T1 core |
| | collocations (morae estimated) | 5,854 · 2.6 h | T3 ear |
| | `word + が` for `pitchConfidence: high` | 1,266 · 0.4 h | T3 ear |
| | `definitionJa` | 4,924 · 6.4 h | T3 ear |

Connected speech ≈ **19.6 h** distinct (mined 15.7 h + pieces 3.9 h); drill clips ≈ **11.6 h**; ≈ **31.1 h distinct, ≈ 33.9 h rendered** with the slow takes (story lines 0.8 h + grammar 0.4 h + pool vocab Stations 4–17 1.3 h + pieces 0.4 h).

|---|---|---|

## 4. Segmenter calibration

`linkSentence` (the segmenter the example sentences are judged by) against the stories' own `tokenIds` (3,268 lines of the texts, each story's cumulative allowlist + its plusOne words): the all-known verdict agrees on 94.3 % of lines, the linked id set is identical on 58.0 %, the vocab ids alone on 63.6 %. The differences are the writers' tokenisation of grammar surfaces (とても, ように, のため kept whole or split) and the V20 matcher's hint rule on unknown verbs (歩きました counts as known through the ました hint in a story; the segmenter keeps 歩き unknown). Story lines enter the pools by their writers' `tokenIds`, not by this segmenter, so the pools are not affected by the disagreement.

## 5. Vocab examples outside every pool (Pass 4b rewrite candidates)

4,065 examples hold a token that links nowhere in the corpus (shown in brackets); they are rendered only once rewritten. Items without an own-station all-known example (the V46 list, 1,244 items) follow, with each example's unknown tokens against the item's own station.
